- **Sprint 100 (2026-08-19)** — Phase-F review-close correctness bundle: mark-to-market equity + NaN-probs guards + non-positive exit-price guard + absolute-drawdown fallback + NaN raw-target skip. Six review items (§4.1, §4.3, §4.5, §6.4, §6.5, plus the Trade dataclass extension mark-to-market required) landed in one arc under hard rule 6. `Trade` dataclass gains `entry_price: float` and `exit_price: float` — the walker knows both at construction, and downstream mark-to-market needs both. `compute_pnl` gains a symmetric non-positive/NaN `exit_price` guard mirroring the entry-price guard. `variance_vn` and `derive_prediction_scalars` raise `ValueError` when `probs` contain NaN — softmax on NaN logits would otherwise silently poison every downstream sum. `max_drawdown_from_equity` gains an absolute-drawdown fallback: when `peak_at_trough <= 0` (all-loss curve), report `|drawdowns[trough] / equity[trough]|` instead of returning 0. A strategy that lost every bar no longer reports "0% drawdown." `run_simulation_with_trades` gains a `fill_price > 0` skip alongside the existing `vol <= 0` skip, and increments a new `n_bars_skipped_nan_price` counter on `SimResult`. `build_equity_curve` gains a keyword-only `prices: np.ndarray | None = None`: when provided (length must equal `bar_timestamps_utc`), each open interval `[entry_bar_idx, exit_bar_idx)` carries `signed_size * (prices[t] - entry_price) / entry_price` as unrealized P&L; at `exit_bar_idx` the closed `pnl_net` posts. When `None`, the Sprint 097 step-function form ships unchanged (backwards compatible). `compute_sim_metrics` gains the same `prices` kwarg; the walker threads `prices_slice[context_len : n_rows - 1].cpu().numpy()`. Tests +10: symmetric `exit_price` guards (non-positive + NaN); NaN-probs raises on `variance_vn` + `derive_prediction_scalars`; all-loss curve reports non-zero drawdown; hand-computed 5-bar mark-to-market equity matches expected `[0, 50k, 100k, 80k, 120k]` on `prices=[100, 105, 110, 108, 112]` with `size=$1M`; step-function backwards-compat; 30-bar linspace rising price yields non-zero mark-to-market Sharpe (dispersion carried across the hold); NaN raw-target skip preserves the counter path; walker-constructed trades expose `entry_price` + `exit_price`. Two pre-existing tests updated to add the new dataclass fields. Test count 585 → 595. Live smoke on real 2015-2022 corpus 2000-row slice with xs+8-step checkpoint (same configuration as Sprint 097's smoke): mark-to-market Sharpe = **-0.8965** (vs Sprint 097's step-function 0.0), `sharpe_se=0.4963`, `positive_fraction=0.0435`, `max_drawdown=3.7772`, `time_to_recovery=968`, `hit_rate=0.0`, `pnl_total=-$33,525.89`. The pre-registered Sharpe/Sharpe-SE/positive-fraction gates now consume tech-arch §11.5's intended continuous mark-to-market metric instead of the Sprint 097 step-function approximation. The negative Sharpe on the untrained-checkpoint single-trade condition is correct: the short position accumulates real per-bar losses across 1935 bars, and the continuous walk sees them. `max_drawdown=3.77` (>100%) is honest math on a degenerate ledger — the peak-to-trough loss exceeded the modest positive peak. A Sprint 084 GPU-trained model with reversals produces well-formed drawdown ratios. Files touched: 5 code (`positions.py`, `metrics.py`, `skeleton.py`, `policy.py`, `prediction.py`) + 1 test file (new, 10 tests) + 1 pre-existing test file patched (2 fixtures). Ruff, mypy, pytest all green. **Deferred to Phase G refactor arc:** §2 walker-hooks refactor, §2 SimResult discriminated union, §2 extract position helpers to `positions.py`, §3.1 vectorize `derive_prices_from_log_returns`, §3.3 `searchsorted` in `build_equity_curve`, §3.4 cached `train_means_tensor`, §6.2 shared axis-sweep helper. **Skipped (named behavior):** §4.2 fill-price approximation (documented on Sprint 096 card), §4.4 strict `sharpe_crosses_zero_in_range` (spec-named Layer-7 gate), §6.3 `BARS_PER_YEAR_DEFAULT=6552` (low drift risk under current bar cadence), §6.6 bootstrap seed default (over-engineered as-is). `pass_kind: functional`, `determinism_budget: bit-deterministic`.

- **Sprint 099 (2026-08-18)** — Kappa sensitivity plot + SENSITIVITY_PLOT_GENERATED (Phase F closes, roadmap 078). Ships the pre-registered Layer-7 gate flag `sharpe_crosses_zero_in_range` from product-spec § 'Success gates for v1' — a robust reported number requires this flag be False on the held-out sim. New `src/price_space_llm/simulation/sensitivity.py`: `SensitivityPoint` frozen dataclass (`kappa_multiplier`, `sharpe`, `sharpe_se`); `SensitivityPlotResult` frozen dataclass (`points`, `min_sharpe`, `max_sharpe`, `sharpe_crosses_zero_in_range: bool`, `per_multiplier_results`); `run_kappa_sensitivity(artifact, model, bucket_stats, policy_template, kappa_multipliers, *, target_symbol, emitter, run_id, ...) -> SensitivityPlotResult`. Per multiplier `m`: `policy = replace(policy_template, slippage_frac=policy_template.slippage_frac * m)`; sub_run_id = `f"{run_id}-kappa-{m:0.4f}"`; nested `run_simulation_with_trades` fires its own SIM_RUN pair; extract `metrics.sharpe_point` + `metrics.sharpe_se`. Zero-cross rule: `crosses = (min_sharpe < 0.0) AND (max_sharpe > 0.0)` — strict both sides; a flat-Sharpe-zero sweep does not "cross" (this is what the smoke exercises). Emits `SENSITIVITY_PLOT_GENERATED` once with `run_id`, `kappa_multipliers: list<float>`, `sharpe_by_multiplier: list<float>`, `min_sharpe`, `max_sharpe`, `sharpe_crosses_zero_in_range`. **Kappa linearization named on card + module:** Sprint 096 established `slippage_frac × |size|` as the linearization of `kappa × size²`; scaling `slippage_frac` by `m` scales the linearized kappa cost by `m` at fixed size. That is the mechanic the sweep exercises. Empty `kappa_multipliers` raises `ValueError` — fail-loud contract. Tests +7: one-point-per-multiplier; zero-crossing True on stubbed schedule `[0.4, 0.2, 0.05, -0.1, -0.3]`; zero-crossing False when all points positive; zero-crossing False when all points negative; one `SENSITIVITY_PLOT_GENERATED` per sweep with the required six payload keys; N SIM_RUN pairs per N-multiplier sweep; empty multipliers raises. Test count 578 → 585. Live smoke on real 2015-2022 corpus 2000-row slice with xs+8-step checkpoint (s=8 latest), 6-multiplier sweep `[0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0]`, baseline policy `(threshold=0.001, hysteresis=0.05, spread=5bps, slippage=2bps)`: emit trace 6 SIM_RUN_STARTED + 6 SIM_RUN_COMPLETED + 1 SENSITIVITY_PLOT_GENERATED (exact). Every per-multiplier Sharpe = 0.0; `sharpe_crosses_zero_in_range = False`. The False flag on an all-zero surface is honest — the strict `(min<0 AND max>0)` guard excludes flat-zero from crossing (a robust reported number requires positive Sharpe across the range; a flat-zero sweep is neither robust nor crossing, it is silent). Same untrained-checkpoint condition as Sprints 097/098: constant-sign edge over 2000 bars → one trade → zero-std bar-pnl → Sharpe pinned at zero by the guard, invariant under multiplier scaling. Sprint 084's GPU-trained model will exercise the crossing math on non-flat surfaces. Files touched: 2 code (`sensitivity.py` new; `simulation/__init__.py` exports) + 1 test file (new, 7 tests). Ruff, mypy, pytest all green. **Phase F closes.** Roadmap 074 → 092, 075 → 093/094/096, 076 → 097, 077 → 098, 078 → 099. Phase G guards mostly landed; Phase H opens next — rented-GPU sweeps against the six-axis dispatch space (size, context_len, mixer, patch, quantile head, n_buckets). `pass_kind: functional`, `determinism_budget: bit-deterministic`.

- **Sprint 098 (2026-08-18)** — Capacity sweep + CAPACITY_SWEEP_COMPLETED (Phase F, roadmap 077). Closes the seventh `SIM_RUN_COMPLETED.implied_capacity_at_this_size_usd` field's meaning: the sweep, not any single sim run, computes capacity. Six-of-seven filled per sim run remains true; capacity emerges at the sweep level. New `src/price_space_llm/simulation/capacity.py`: `CapacityPoint` frozen dataclass (`size_usd`, `sharpe`, `sharpe_se`); `CapacitySweepResult` frozen dataclass (`points: tuple[CapacityPoint, ...]`, `capacity_usd: float`, `sharpe_at_capacity: float`, `per_size_results: tuple[SimResult, ...]`); `run_capacity_sweep(artifact, model, bucket_stats, policy_template, sizes_usd, *, target_symbol, emitter, run_id, checkpoint_run_id, cost_calibration_run_id, split, date_range_start, date_range_end) -> CapacitySweepResult`. Per size: sub_run_id = `f"{run_id}-cap-{int(size):012d}"`; `dataclasses.replace(policy_template, position_size_usd=size)` overrides the size knob only; nested `run_simulation_with_trades` fires its own `SIM_RUN_STARTED` + `SIM_RUN_COMPLETED`; the sweep extracts `metrics.sharpe_point` + `metrics.sharpe_se`. Capacity rule: `capacity_usd = max(size for point in points if point.sharpe > 0)`; if no point crossed zero → `capacity_usd = 0.0` (honest zero, not a fabricated ceiling — named on card). Emits `CAPACITY_SWEEP_COMPLETED` once at close with `run_id`, `points: list<struct<size_usd, sharpe, sharpe_se>>`, `capacity_usd`, `sharpe_at_capacity`. Vocabulary v0.2's `struct` composite kind carries the payload without new vocab pressure. Empty `sizes_usd` raises `ValueError` — fail-loud contract, no silent no-op. Tests +8: one-point-per-size shape; `capacity_usd` = largest positive-Sharpe size (stubbed sim with sharpe>0 at [1M, 3M] and sharpe<0 at [10M] → capacity=$3M); all-negative → capacity=0; all-positive → capacity=max(sizes); one `CAPACITY_SWEEP_COMPLETED` per sweep + payload key set; N SIM_RUN pairs per N-size sweep; empty sizes raises; `CapacityPoint` frozen (mutation raises). Test count 570 → 578. Live smoke on real 2015-2022 corpus 2000-row slice with xs+8-step checkpoint (s=8 latest), 5-size sweep `[100K, 300K, 1M, 3M, 10M]`, policy `(threshold=0.001, hysteresis=0.05, spread=5bps, slippage=2bps)`: 5 SIM_RUN_STARTED + 5 SIM_RUN_COMPLETED + 1 CAPACITY_SWEEP_COMPLETED emit trace (exact). Every per-size Sharpe = 0.0 with SE = 0.0; `capacity_usd = $0`. Zero-Sharpe outcome is the honest tail of the same monotone-loss single-trade condition Sprint 097's smoke exposed: the untrained s=8 checkpoint produces constant-sign edge over 2000 bars → one trade → zero-std bar-pnl → Sharpe=0 by the zero-std guard. Scaling `position_size_usd` from $100K to $10M scales pnl linearly, leaves Sharpe invariant — the null-hypothesis outcome the code correctly reports. A Sprint 084 GPU-trained model with direction reversals produces non-trivial per-size Sharpe surfaces and a meaningful `capacity_usd`. - **Sprint 097 (2026-08-18)** — Simulator metrics + block bootstrap on the trade ledger (Phase F, roadmap 076). Six of the seven `SIM_RUN_COMPLETED` payload fields now populate from live math instead of Sprint 096's zeros. Only `implied_capacity_at_this_size_usd` stays at 0 until Sprint 098's capacity sweep. New `src/price_space_llm/simulation/metrics.py`: `SimMetrics` dataclass (`sharpe_point`, `sharpe_se`, `block_bootstrap_positive_fraction`, `max_drawdown`, `time_to_recovery_bars`, `hit_rate`, `n_bars`, `n_trades`). `build_equity_curve(trades, bar_timestamps_utc) -> Tensor[T]` uses `bisect_right` on trade exit timestamps — `equity[t]` advances at the first bar with `bar_ts > exit_ts`. `compute_sharpe(bar_pnl, bars_per_year=6552)` — zero-std guard returns 0.0 without NaN. `block_bootstrap_sharpe(bar_pnl, *, n_blocks=18, n_resamples=10_000, bars_per_block=546, rng=None)` — partitions bar_pnl into non-overlapping 546-bar monthly blocks, per resample picks `n_blocks` block indices with replacement, concatenates, computes Sharpe; `(SE, positive_fraction)`. Deterministic given `numpy.random.Generator` seed. `max_drawdown_from_equity(equity) -> (max_dd_frac, trough_index)` — `max(cummax - equity) / max(cummax, eps)`; ties broken toward earliest trough. `time_to_recovery_bars_from_equity(equity, trough_index)` — bars from trough to first re-touch of pre-drawdown peak; unrecovered runs return `bars_remaining` (honest count, not sentinel). `compute_sim_metrics(trades, bar_timestamps_utc, *, bars_per_year=6552, n_resamples=10_000, n_blocks=18, bars_per_block=546, rng_seed=42)` orchestrator. `SimResult` extended with `metrics: SimMetrics | None`. `run_simulation_with_trades` builds `processed_bar_timestamps` for `t ∈ [context_len, n_rows-1)`, calls `compute_sim_metrics`, populates the emit payload from the result. Named approximations documented in module + card: equity counts closed trades only (mark-to-market of currently-open positions ignored; Sprint 099+ may refine); production-side default `n_resamples=10_000` matches spec § 11.5, tests use smaller for speed. Tests +14: two `build_equity_curve` (step at each exit; empty ledger returns zeros); three `compute_sharpe` (zero-std, empty, hand-computed with sqrt(6552)*mean/std); three `max_drawdown` (monotone → 0, V-shape → 0.5 at trough, empty); two `time_to_recovery` (recovers at trough+1, unrecovered returns bars_remaining); two `block_bootstrap_sharpe` (reproducible with seed, short-series → zeros); two `compute_sim_metrics` (empty ledger, three-trade fixture with hit_rate=2/3 and max_dd=0.5). Test count 556 → 570. Live smoke on real 2015-2022 corpus 2000-row slice with xs+10-step checkpoint, policy `(threshold=0.001, hysteresis=0.05, spread=5bps, slippage=2bps)`: 1935 bars processed, 1 trade (untrained model produces constant-sign edge over full window; no direction reversals across 2000 bars). All six metric fields populate cleanly: `sharpe_point=0`, `sharpe_se=0`, `positive_fraction=0`, `max_drawdown=0`, `time_to_recovery=1`, `hit_rate=0`, `pnl_total=-$33,525.89`. Single-trade equity monotonically decreases into the loss (never peaks and drops → dd=0); this is real-math output, not fabrication. Synthetic three-trade test proves the metrics compute correctly on multi-trade ledgers; a Sprint 084+ GPU-trained model will produce many reversals and full metric ranges. - **Sprint 096 (2026-08-18)** — Position state machine + trades (Phase F, roadmap 075 part 3). Three new emit sites fire from live code for the first time. New `src/price_space_llm/simulation/positions.py`: `Position` dataclass (direction, size, entry_bar_ts_utc, entry_price, entry_bar_index); `Trade` dataclass (trade_id, entry/exit ts, direction, size, pnl_gross, pnl_net, cost_spread, cost_slippage, hold_duration_bars); `compute_pnl(size, entry_price, exit_price, spread_cost_frac, slippage_frac) -> (gross, net, cost_spread, cost_slippage)` — signed-size formula `pnl_gross = size × (exit/entry − 1)` encodes long/short in one line, raises on non-positive `entry_price`; `derive_prices_from_log_returns(raw_targets)` cumulative anchored at 1.0 via `p_t = p_{t-1} × exp(log_return_t)` with NaN entries holding the last valid price. New `run_simulation_with_trades(...)` extends the Sprint 094 walker with position-state transitions on any change from `current_direction`: close existing at fill=t+1's price → emit POSITION_CLOSED + TRADE_LEDGERED with `close_kind="normal"`; open new at fill=t+1 → emit POSITION_OPENED. End-of-range flush: if a position is still open when the walker exits, force-close at the last bar's price with `close_kind="sim_range_end"`. Refuses when `artifact.raw_targets is None` (Sprint 088 field is a hard requirement). Walker halts at `t = n_rows - 2` because a decision at `t` requires bar `t+1` as fill. `SimResult` extended: `trades: tuple[Trade, ...]` + `pnl_total: float`. Named approximations documented in module + sprint card: **fill price = close at fill bar** (spec's "next bar's open" unavailable from the tokenizer; the approximation applies uniformly to entries + exits and cancels in the round-trip); **single `spread_cost_frac` = round-trip cost fraction** (equivalent to half-spread on each leg under symmetric assumptions); **`slippage_frac × |size|` linearizes `kappa × size²`** (Sprint 099's kappa sensitivity plot revisits the quadratic form). - **Sprint 087 (2026-08-17)** — `PatchEmbedder` (patch_size=4) end-to-end per tech-arch § 10. Third of the four Phase E ablation embedders. `MarketStateTransformerConfig.patch_size: int = 1` (Literal {1, 4}; other values raise); `MarketStateTransformer.__init__` picks embedder: patch_size==1 → uses `fusion` axis (sum/mixer); patch_size>1 → PatchEmbedder (overrides fusion since patching is orthogonal to per-timestep fusion). Position embedding sized to `context_len // patch_size`. `PatchEmbedder` per-channel `nn.Linear(patch_size * F_c, d_model)` summed after reshape `[B, T, F_c]` → `[B, T // patch, patch * F_c]`; raises on `T % patch_size != 0` and on unexpected channel keys. Trainer target alignment: new `_patch_targets(targets, patch_size)` helper = `targets[:, patch-1::patch]` (patched-position p corresponds to sampler-slice index `(p+1)*patch - 1`); applied in both train + val paths. `scripts/train.py --patch-size {1,4}` + `-p4` run_id infix + stderr shape line names the patch size. Tests +7 (embedder output shape; rejects non-divisible T; rejects extra channels; MarketStateTransformer with patch=4 outputs `[B, 16, V]` on `context_len=64`; rejects unknown patch_size; rejects indivisible context_len; end-to-end synthetic .pt CLI smoke with `-p4` infix). Test count 479 → 486. Live smoke on real 2015-2022 corpus at xs + patch=4: exits 0 in 0.05s at 848,256 params (28K more than sum-fusion's 819,840, from the 4× wider linear projection), loss 3.5543. Patch=4 does NOT trip the mixer's divergence guard on un-normalized features because packing amortizes the input scale across four bars. Trace directory carries `-p4-` infix. Idempotent: existing sidecars skipped; existing index rows detected by `cache_key` and skipped. Live backfill: dry-run confirmed 11 candidates; real run wrote 11 sidecars + 11 index rows; post-backfill `find data/raw -name "*.meta.json" | wc -l` = 11,119 (matches `find data/raw -name "*.json" -not -name "*.meta.json" | wc -l`); `wc -l data/raw/cache_index.jsonl` = 11,119 (matches file count exactly). - **Sprint 083 (2026-08-17)** — `ChannelMixerEmbedder` channel-aware fusion embedder per tech-arch § 10 code sketch. Roadmap item 069 (Phase E ablation infrastructure). Sibling to Sprint 053's linear-sum `MarketStateEmbedder`; same interface (`dict[str, Tensor[B, T, F_c]] → Tensor[B, T, d_model]`) so `MarketStateTransformer` can swap embedders via a `fusion: str = "sum"` field on `MarketStateTransformerConfig`. Implementation faithful to the spec code sketch: per-channel `nn.Linear(F_c, mixer_dim)`, `nn.MultiheadAttention(mixer_dim, n_heads, batch_first=True)` across the channel axis at each timestep (bidirectional — channels have no order; time causality is preserved by the outer temporal transformer), mean-pool across channels, `nn.Linear(mixer_dim, d_model)`. Deterministic channel iteration via `self.channel_order` frozen at construction. `MarketStateTransformer.__init__` raises `ValueError` on unknown fusion strings. `scripts/train.py` gains `--fusion {sum,mixer}` + `--mixer-dim N` + `--mixer-n-heads N`; `run_id` picks up a `-mix` infix when `fusion=mixer` so per-fusion checkpoints and traces do not collide. Tests +6 (shape; extra-channel raises; deterministic-with-seed; fusion=mixer swaps embedder; fusion=wrong raises; end-to-end synthetic .pt CLI smoke with `-mix` infix). Test count 464 → 470. Files touched: 3 code (`model/transformer.py`, `model/__init__.py`, `scripts/train.py`) + 2 test files. Ruff, mypy, pytest all green. Live smoke sum vs mixer at xs on the 2015-2022 training .pt: sum exits 0 in 0.08s at 819,840 params (loss 3.6837); **mixer diverges at step 1** with `grad_norm ≈ 33.5M` even at `--lr 1e-5`. Divergence is a property of the pre-clip gradient scale, not the learning rate — the trainer's `grad_norm > grad_clip*10 = 10` guard fires on the first backprop through the mixer's random-init attention on the un-normalized real corpus (dollar_volume ~$448M, VXX volume ~10^5). Sum-fusion tolerates the same features because the transformer's internal `LayerNorm` absorbs the scale mismatch. The mixer needs a normalizer-applied .pt to train stably. Filed to drift-watchlist. Sprint 083 test smoke uses a synthetic small-scale .pt (features drawn from N(0, 0.1)) to verify the code path executes end-to-end; production mixer runs (Sprint 087 architecture ablation) must consume `scripts/bucketize.py --fit-normalizer` output. **Named on card:** v0.7 vocab candidate exposing `fusion / mixer_dim / mixer_n_heads` on `CONFIG_RESOLVED` — currently the fusion axis is recoverable from `run_id` + git SHA, not from the trace payload alone; drift-watchlist entry filed alongside the v0.6 candidate for `d_model / n_layers / n_heads`. `pass_kind: architecture`, `determinism_budget: statistically-deterministic`.

- **Sprint 082 (2026-08-17)** — Evaluator ignore-index filter in `_metrics_over_positions`. Real correctness gap on the market-state feats path: `TokenizedArtifact.targets` (Sprint 052) carries -100 for null log-return rows; `_metrics_over_positions` in `src/price_space_llm/evaluation/evaluate.py` passed last-position targets straight into `compute_metric_set` without stripping the sentinel. `compute_nll` invoked `probs.gather(1, targets.unsqueeze(1))` with target=-100 picking the last row's probability silently; `compute_top_k` compared preds against -100 and always missed (deflating hit rate); `compute_brier` scattered to an invalid row; `compute_dir_acc` returned False for `-100 >= threshold`. All wrong. Fix: new `IGNORE_INDEX = -100` module constant; `_metrics_over_positions` filters `last_targets != IGNORE_INDEX` before invoking `compute_metric_set`; empty-after-filter path returns a zero-metric `MetricSet` with `n_examples=0`. Test count 460 → 462. Files touched: 1 code (`evaluation/evaluate.py`) + 1 test file. Ruff, mypy, pytest all green. Bucket-ID legacy path unaffected (tokens list is null-stripped upstream, so -100 never landed there). `pass_kind: functional`, `determinism_budget: bit-deterministic`.

A new pipeline stage that mints a fresh dated-filename shape must register its pattern in `KNOWN_DATED_PATTERNS` — the fail-loud raise forces the addition to be visible at review time. - **Sprint 077 (2026-08-17)** — Fail-loud loads for legacy artifacts + roadmap errata block. Architect surfaced two loosened SDD invariants after the Sprint 075-076 close: (a) `load_tokens_pt` silently accepted `.pt` files with no `mask_semantics` marker — a pre-Sprint-075 artifact returned all-False masks without a signal; (b) `load_frozen_normalizer` silently fell back to `STD_CLAMP=1e-8` when the persisted `std_clamp` key was absent. Existing test fixture `_sample_payload` in `tests/test_model.py` gained a `mask_semantics` kwarg (default = `"target_valid_v075"`) so the three prior `load_tokens_pt` roundtrip tests continue to test what they intended; `tests/test_evaluator.py::test_run_evaluation_feats_end_to_end` payload updated to carry the marker. `normalizer/__init__.py` exports `LegacyStdClampMissing`. - **Sprint 076 (2026-08-17)** — `std_clamp` promoted to a `FrozenNormalizer` field + `warn_channels_under_clamp` helper. Closes the 2026-08-16 § 11 drift-watchlist entry: `apply_frozen_normalizer` used a module-level `STD_CLAMP = 1e-8`; a future engineered feature whose genuine std sat inside `[1e-9, 1e-7]` would silently over-normalize. Fix: `FrozenNormalizer` gains `std_clamp: float = 1e-8` as a keyword-only field; `apply_frozen_normalizer` reads `normalizer.std_clamp` instead of the module constant; `write_frozen_normalizer` payload adds the `std_clamp` key; `load_frozen_normalizer` reads it with fallback to `STD_CLAMP` for pre-Sprint-076 artifacts on disk. New `EXPECTED_CONSTANT_CHANNELS = frozenset({"event__FOMC", "event__CPI_RELEASE", "event__OPTIONS_EXPIRY", "event__EARNINGS_DENSITY_SPX"})` — the four event channels are always-zero-std by construction; extending the set requires a code change so the exemption is auditable. New `warn_channels_under_clamp(normalizer, *, exempt=EXPECTED_CONSTANT_CHANNELS) -> list[(channel, feature_index, std_value)]` — opt-in check; caller decides whether to log / raise / continue. `scripts/measure_drift.py` wiring deferred to a follow-up sprint per hard rule 6. Tests +4: default clamp = 1e-8 on a bare `FrozenNormalizer`; per-normalizer clamp of 1.0 on a constant column overrides the module 1e-8 (result stays at input value instead of blowing up to 3e8); write→load round-trip preserves clamp = 1e-6; helper reports `[("target__SPY", 3, ~1e-10)]` for a normalizer with `event__FOMC` (exempt, std=0) + `target__SPY` (`std[3]=1e-10`) and does not include the FOMC hit. Test count 445 → 449. Files touched: 2 code (`normalizer/frozen.py`, `normalizer/__init__.py`) + 1 test file. Ruff, mypy, pytest all green. Follow-ups named on card: wire `warn_channels_under_clamp` into `scripts/measure_drift.py` at fit time; v0.6 vocab candidate `NORMALIZER_CHANNEL_UNDER_CLAMP` for structured signal-side reporting. `pass_kind: architecture`, `determinism_budget: bit-deterministic`.

- **Sprint 075 (2026-08-17)** — Mask redefinition to target-valid semantic. Closes the Sprint 052 open item the 2026-08-16 review-verify surfaced. Old semantic: `mask[t] = all_horizontal(is_not_null across every feature column across every channel)` — collapsed to all-False on the real corpus because sparse event channels (options-expiry ~12/year, monthly-to-quarterly macros) poisoned the check on every 15-minute bar. New semantic: `mask[t] = target_features_non_null[t] AND targets[t] != -100`. Target key computed as `f"target__{target_symbol}"`; non-target null columns do not depress the flag (the MarketStateEmbedder already handles nulls as 0.0 tensors at tokenize time). `run_tokenizer_pt` in `src/price_space_llm/tokenizer/bucketize.py` computes the new mask block; `meta["mask_semantics"] = "target_valid_v075"` version marker added so a future consumer can distinguish pre- vs post-Sprint-075 artifacts. `TokenizedArtifact.mask` docstring at `src/price_space_llm/model/dataset.py:128` updated. Regenerated the training .pt via `scripts/bucketize.py --features data/features/features-align-2015-01-2022-12-….parquet --training-start 2015-01-02 --training-end 2022-12-30 --format pt`: 54,262 rows, mask.sum() = 53,703 (98.97%), targets != -100 = 54,235 (99.95%), delta 532 = rows where the label is present but a target feature (rolling window burn-in) is null. `meta.mask_semantics = "target_valid_v075"` on the regenerated .pt. Verified downstream: Sprint 073/074 xs+c128 smoke on the regenerated .pt exits 0, full training tag surface fires, param count 828,032 in 0.11s (was 827,392 on the pre-regen .pt — delta 640 = 5 × 128 from a channel that landed in the current-code features parquet). Tests +3: synthetic 4-row truth table `[True, False, False, False]` across the 2×2 (target-features-valid × target-label-present) grid; non-target-nulls do not depress mask (3-row synthetic with all-null `market_context__QQQ__log_return` reads `[True, True, True]`); regenerated real-corpus training .pt reports `mask.sum() > 0` AND `meta["mask_semantics"] == "target_valid_v075"`. Test count 442 → 445. Files touched: 2 code (`tokenizer/bucketize.py`, `model/dataset.py`) + 1 test file. Ruff, mypy, pytest all green. Test-window .pt regen deferred (would trip the `heldout_guard` test-look budget; test-window mask fix lands with Sprint 088 held-out evaluation prep instead). Named on close: `WindowSamplerFeats` still ignores `mask`; gating on it is a separate concern for a Sprint 088+ candidate. `pass_kind: functional`, `determinism_budget: bit-deterministic`.

- **Sprint 074 (2026-08-17)** — Config-driven context lengths (Phase E, roadmap 068). Sibling shape to Sprint 073. Four spec-pre-registered values land as `configs/context/{64,128,256,512}.json` — each a single-key `{"context_len": N}` file. New `ContextLenConfig` in `src/price_space_llm/config.py` — Pydantic v2 with `Literal[64, 128, 256, 512]` so an off-spec value raises at parse (`context_len=100` rejected in `test_context_len_config_rejects_off_spec_value`). `scripts/train.py` gains `--context-size {64,128,256,512}` and `--context-config PATH`, mutually exclusive. When present, overlays `cfg.context_len` on the `ExperimentConfig` via `cfg.__class__.model_validate({**cfg.model_dump(), "context_len": N})` — re-runs Pydantic validation so a hand-authored off-spec value fails loud even if it slipped through `ContextLenConfig.model_validate`. `run_id` picks up a `-c{N}` infix that composes with Sprint 073's `-{size}`; per-context checkpoints and traces do not collide on disk. Stderr prints the resolved `context_len` at session open. Live sweep on the real 2015-2022 training .pt at `--model-size xs` × every context: c64 → params 819,200 in 0.08s (loss 3.6311); c128 → 827,392 in 0.11s (3.5735); c256 → 843,776 in 0.17s (3.5678); c512 → 876,544 in 0.36s (3.5499). Position-embedding parameter arithmetic verifies exactly: `Δparams = Δcontext_len × d_model` on each step (64 × 128 = 8,192; 128 × 128 = 16,384; 256 × 128 = 32,768). Wall clock scales sub-linearly (0.08 → 0.36s over 8× context). Trace directory names carry both `-xs-` and `-c{N}-` infix — the two Phase E dispatch axes compose. Full training tag surface fires on every sweep point. Tests +5 (off-spec rejected; every canonical file parses with value=filename; round-trip fidelity; CLI mutex on --context-size and --context-config; end-to-end xs+c128 smoke asserts stderr `context_len=128` and the composed infix). - **Sprint 064 (2026-08-16)** — `check_test_look.sh` pre-commit hook (commit-msg stage). Closes review § 3 aggregate-audit MEDIUM item (tech-arch §13 filesystem-date guard). Sprint 051 wired the runtime guard in `scripts/evaluate.py`; Sprint 064 adds the commit-time guard. New `src/price_space_llm/hooks.py`: pure-Python `check_commit_for_test_look(commit_message, staged_files, config_globs)` returning `(should_block, reason)`. Blocks iff any staged file matches `configs/**/*.json`, `configs/**/*.yaml`, `configs/**/*.yml`, or `data/manifests/**/*.json` AND contains a `YYYY-MM-DD` date with `YYYY >= 2024` AND the commit message lacks the `[test-look]` token. `scripts/check_test_look.sh` bash wrapper — install via `ln -sf "$(pwd)/scripts/check_test_look.sh" .git/hooks/commit-msg`; resolves staged files via `git diff --cached --name-only`. Regex uses lookahead/lookbehind on digits (`(?<!\d)(20[2-9][0-9])-([01][0-9])-([0-3][0-9])(?!\d)`) so ISO timestamps like `2025-06-30T20:00:00` match — the `T` after `30` is a word character and breaks `\b`-boundary semantics. Uses `PurePath.full_match` (Python 3.13+) so `data/manifests/**/*.json` matches a file directly under the directory. - **Sprint 062 (2026-08-16)** — Session flags: `is_overnight_gap` + cyclic minute-of-day + cyclic day-of-week. Closes review § 3 aggregate-audit MEDIUM item ("session flags absent") and fills Sprint 052's `is_overnight_gap=None` placeholder. New `src/price_space_llm/tokenizer/session.py`: `compute_session_features(timestamps: Tensor[T]) -> (Tensor[T, 4], Tensor[T] int8)`. Columns `[minute_of_day_sin, minute_of_day_cos, day_of_week_sin, day_of_week_cos]` — continuous cyclic encoding so the model sees no discontinuity at midnight or week rollover. `is_overnight_gap[i] = 1` iff `timestamps[i] - timestamps[i-1] > BAR_SECONDS` (first bar of a new session); row 0 = 1 by convention. Timezone-agnostic (works on UTC without ET conversion). Wired into `run_tokenizer_pt`: adds `session__flags` channel (shape `[T, 4]`) to `features` dict + populates `is_overnight_gap` field in the .pt payload (previously `None`). Tests +5 (shape + bounds; overnight detection with a 17-hour gap between bars 1 and 2; sin/cos at midnight = (0, 1); sin/cos at noon = (0, -1); empty timestamps; tokenizer end-to-end writes both fields). - **Sprint 057 (2026-08-15)** — Trainer: AdamW + cosine LR + warmup + bf16 (opt-in) + deterministic. Spec § 9.2 explicit on all four"). `TrainerConfig` gains `warmup_steps=2000`, `lr_min_frac=0.1`, `weight_decay=0.1`, `betas=(0.9, 0.95)`, `bf16=False`, `deterministic=True`. New helpers: `build_adamw_with_param_groups` (2D+ params get weight_decay; 1D biases + LayerNorm gains get 0.0 per standard GPT-family recipe), `cosine_with_warmup_lr` (linear warmup then cosine to `base_lr * min_frac`). `run_training_feats` wires all four: AdamW replaces Adam; per-step LR override via cosine+warmup; bf16 autocast when `bf16=True` OR `device.type == "cuda"`; `torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True, warn_only=True)` + `CUBLAS_WORKSPACE_CONFIG=:4096:8` env var set when `deterministic=True`. `scripts/train.py` adds `--warmup-steps`, `--weight-decay`, `--lr-min-frac`, `--bf16`, `--no-deterministic` flags. `TRAINING_STEP_COMPLETED.lr` now carries the per-step scheduled value (was constant `trainer_cfg.lr`). Legacy `run_training` (bucket-ID) stays on Adam for baseline back-compat. Files touched: 3 (trainer.py, scripts/train.py, tests/test_trainer.py). Tests +3: param-group split (Linear weight → decay, Linear bias + LayerNorm → no-decay); cosine warmup shape (0 at step 0, base_lr at warmup_steps, base_lr*min_frac at total_steps); 4-step smoke with warmup=2 producing distinct per-step LRs. Test count 373 → 376. Ruff + mypy + pytest all green. Live smoke on real training .pt (device=mps, 4 steps in 0.47s, params 212,992, final_train_loss 3.69). `pass_kind: functional`, `determinism_budget: statistically-deterministic`.

- **Sprint 056 (2026-08-15)** — Extended `bucket_stats.json` schema per spec § 7.1. Closes `reviews/full-review-sprints-041-050.md` § 3 aggregate-audit HIGH item ("bucket_stats.json schema is wrong. Simulator's decode of outer buckets to expected return requires bucket_train_mean; baselines.py currently fakes it from edge midpoints"). New `BucketRow` dataclass (lower, upper, train_mean, train_median, train_frequency); `BucketStats.per_bucket: tuple[BucketRow, ...]` field. `fit_bucketizer` computes per-bucket stats via `_compute_per_bucket_stats` after edge dedup (bin against final edges; mean via stdlib; median via `statistics.median`; frequency = count/total). Write path uses `allow_nan=False` and sanitizes NaN train_mean/train_median + None bounds → strict RFC-8259 JSON (no "Infinity" strings). Load path restores NaN for empty-bucket means. New `compute_magnitude_weights_from_stats(BucketStats)` returns `|train_mean|` weights normalized to mean 1; rejects legacy stats missing per_bucket. Old `compute_magnitude_weights(edges, vocab_size)` kept for back-compat with a docstring note pointing at the new function (retirement deferred to a future cleanup sprint once every caller migrates). Live smoke on training window (54,262 rows): per_bucket length 32; frequency sum 1.0000; bucket 0 `lower=None, train_mean=-0.0061` (outer left tail catching 3.13% of rows); bucket 15 `train_mean=0.0` (dedup-nudge zero-return band); bucket 31 `upper=None, train_mean=+0.0060` (outer right tail, symmetric to bucket 0). Test count 364 → 373 (+9: 6 tokenizer + 3 baseline). Files touched: 4 (tokenizer/bucketize.py, baselines.py, tests/test_tokenizer.py, tests/test_baselines.py). Ruff + mypy + pytest all green. Simulator's decode path (roadmap Sprint 074, Phase F) will consume `bucket_train_mean` for `expected_return = sum(p * bucket_train_mean)`. `pass_kind: functional`, `determinism_budget: bit-deterministic`.

Closes the SEVERE `reviews/full-review-sprints-041-050.md` § 1 gap: the model now consumes per-channel feature tensors instead of bucket-IDs. `MarketStateEmbedder` holds `nn.ModuleDict` of one `nn.Linear(F_c, d_model)` per channel; `forward(feats: dict[str, Tensor[B, T, F_c]]) -> Tensor[B, T, d_model]` applies each linear and sums.
